\"\", needRender = true) {\n        this.link = link;\n        this.fileName = CodeFile.getFileName(link, fileName)\n        this.lang = CodeFile.getLang(this.fileName, lang);\n        this.text = \"\";\n        this.$codeElement = null;\n        this.$preElement = null;\n        this.$root = null;\n\n        this.needRender = needRender;\n        this.rendered = false;\n\n        if (!CodeFile.isInitialized) {\n            CodeFile.isInitialized = true;\n\n            try {\n                hljs.configure({\n                    languages: [] // 禁用所有语言自动检测\n                });\n\n            } catch (error) {\n                console.log('初始化代码高亮时出错：', error);\n            }\n        }\n\n        CodeFile.Instances.push(this);  // 记录实例\n\n    }\n\n    static getFileName(link,fileName = \"\") {\n        return fileName || link.split('/').pop();\n    }\n\n    static getLang(fileName, lang = \"\") {\n        return lang || CodeFile.getLanguageFromExtension(fileName.split('.').pop());\n    }\n\n    async loadFile() {\n        const url = window.SiteAssets ? window.SiteAssets.resolve(this.link) : this.link;\n        return fetch(url)\n            .then(response => {\n                if (!response.ok) {\n                    throw new Error('网络响应失败');\n                }\n                return response.text();\n            }).then(data => {\n                this.text = data;\n            })\n            .catch(error => {\n                console.log('读取文件失败: ' + error.message);\n            });\n    }\n\n    render(anchor) {\n        if (this.rendered) {\n            throw new Error('代码块已渲染');\n        }\n        if (!this.needRender) {\n            throw new Error('代码块不需要渲染');\n        }\n        if (!anchor) {\n            anchor = $(document.currentScript);\n        }\n        this.loadFile().then(() => {\n            let $preElement = $('<pre></pre>');\n            let $codeElement = anchor.before($preElement);\n\n            hljs.highlightBlock(this.$codeElement[0]);\n            this.rendered = true;\n            CodeFile.initLineNumbers();\n            $(document).trigger('CodeFileRendered', [this]);\n        });\n    }\n\n    destroy() {\n        this.needRender = false;\n        this.rendered = false;\n        this.$root.remove();\n    }\n\n    static initLineNumbers() {\n        // 判定是否所有代码块都已经渲染完成\n        CodeFile.Instances.forEach((el) => {\n            if (!el.rendered && el.needRender) {\n                return;\n            }\n        });\n        // 启用行号\n        try {\n            hljs.initLineNumbersOnLoad();\n        }\n        catch (error) {\n            console.log('初始化行号时出错:', error);\n        }\n    }\n\n    static getLanguageFromExtension(extension) {\n        const languageMap = {\n            'c': 'C',\n            'cpp': 'C++',\n            'cc': 'C++',\n            'cxx': 'C++',\n            'cs': 'C#',\n            'java': 'Java',\n            'py': 'Python',\n            'js': 'JavaScript',\n            'ts': 'TypeScript',\n            'html': 'HTML',\n            'htm': 'HTML',\n            'css': createCodeFileTools(codeFile) {\n\n    codeFile.$preElement.wrap(`<figure class=\"code-file\"></figure>`);\n    codeFile.$root = codeFile.$preElement.parent();\n    var $codeFileTools = $('<div class=\"code-file-tools\"></div>');\n    codeFile.$preElement.before($codeFileTools);\n    codeFile.$preElement.css('maxHeight', HIGHLIGHT_SHRINK);\n\n    // 关闭代码块按钮\n    var $closeCodeBlockButton = $('<button class=\"close-code-block-button\" type=\"button\" title=\"close-code-block-button\"></button>').css('backgroundImage', 'url(\"' + CLOSE_CODE_BLOCK_ICON + '\")');\n    $codeFileTools.append($closeCodeBlockButton);\n    if (HIGHLIGHT_SHRINK == 'true') {\n        var $hljs = codeFile.$codeElement.find('.hljs');\n        $closeCodeBlockButton.css('transform', \"rotate(-90deg)\");\n        $hljs.addClass(\"closed\");\n    }\n\n    $closeCodeBlockButton.click(function () {\n        if (!codeFile.$codeElement.hasClass('closed')) {\n            $closeCodeBlockButton.css('transform', \"rotate(-90deg)\");\n            codeFile.$codeElement.addClass(\"closed\");\n        } else {\n            $closeCodeBlockButton.css('transform', \"rotate(0deg)\");\n            codeFile.$codeElement.removeClass(\"closed\");\n        }\n    });\n\n    // 显示文件名\n    $codeFileTools.append($('<span class=\"code-file-name\">' + codeFile.fileName + '</span>'));\n\n    // 复制按钮\n    var $copyButton = $('<button class=\"copy-button\" type=\"button\" title=\"复制代码\"></button>').css('backgroundImage', 'url(\"' + COPY_ICON + '\")');\n    $codeFileTools.append($copyButton);\n\n    $copyButton.click(function () {\n        // 创建已复制提示文字\n        var $span = $('<span class=\"copy-notice\">已复制</span>');\n        $codeFileTools.append($span);\n        // 执行复制操作\n        navigator.clipboard.writeText(codeFile.text).then(function () {\n            // 显示已复制提示，并在 1 秒后隐藏\n            $span.css('opacity', 1);\n            setTimeout(function () {\n                $span.css('opacity', 0);\n                setTimeout(function () {\n                    $span.remove();\n                }, 500);\n            }, 1000);\n        }).catch(function (err) {\n            // 如果复制失败，显示错误信息\n            console.error('Failed to copy text:', err);\n            $span.remove();\n        });\n    });\n\n    // 下载按钮\n    var $downloadButton = $('<button class=\"download-button\" type=\"button\" title=\"下载代码文件\"></button>').css('backgroundImage', 'url(\"' + DOWNLOAD_ICON + '\")');\n    $codeFileTools.append($downloadButton);\n    function downloadFileFromUrl(url, filename) {\n        url = window.SiteAssets ? window.SiteAssets.resolve(url) : url;\n        fetch(url)\n            .then(response => response.blob())\n            .then(blob => {\n                const a = document.createElement('a');\n                const fileURL = URL.createObjectURL(blob);\n                a.href = fileURL;\n                a.download = filename;\n                a.click();\n                URL.revokeObjectURL(fileURL); // 释放 URL 对象\n            })\n            .catch(error => console.error('Error fetching the file:', error));\n    }\n    $downloadButton.click(function () {\n        downloadFileFromUrl(codeFile.link, codeFile.fileName);\n    });\n}\n",

            path: "js/codeFileTree.js",
            defaultSelected: true,
            fileType: "code",
            publicHrefPath: "js/codeFileTree.js",
            codeText: "class CodeFileTreeNode {\n    constructor(id, isFolder, linkOrFolderName, children, lang, fileName, icon, opened = true) {\n        this.id = id;\n        this.isFolder = isFolder;\n        this.opened = opened;\n        if (isFolder) {\n            this.folderName = linkOrFolderName;\n            this.children = children;\n            this.icon = icon || \"far fa-folder\";\n        } else {\n            this.link = linkOrFolderName;\n            this.fileName = CodeFile.getFileName(linkOrFolderName, fileName);\n            this.lang = CodeFile.getLang(linkOrFolderName, lang);\n            this.icon = icon || \"far fa-file-code\";\n            if (children) {\n                throw new Error(\"CodeFileTreeNode should not have children if it is a file.\");\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // 添加转换到 jsTree 数据的方法\n    convertToJsTreeData() {\n        let node;\n        if (this.isFolder) {\n            node = {\n                id: this.id,\n                text: this.folderName,\n                children: [],\n                state: {\n                    opened: this.opened\n                },\n                icon: this.icon,\n                data: {\n                    isFolder: true,\n                },\n            };\n        } else {\n            node = {\n                id: this.id,\n                text: this.fileName,\n                children: [],\n                state: {\n                    opened: this.opened\n                },\n                icon: this.icon,\n                data: {\n                    isFolder: false,\n                    link: this.link,\n                    lang: this.lang,\n                    fileName: this.fileName,\n                }\n            };\n        }\n\n        if (this.children && Array.isArray(this.children)) {\n            node.children = this.children.map(childNode => childNode.convertToJsTreeData());\n        }\n\n        return node;\n    }\n}\n\nclass CodeFileTree {\n    constructor(id, root, defaultNode) {\n        this.id = id;\n        this.root = root;\n        this.defaultNode = defaultNode;\n        this.jsTreeData = \"\";\n        this.codeFile = null;\n        this.jsTree = null;\n        this.container = null;\n    }\n\n    render() {\n        this.jsTreeData = this.root.convertToJsTreeData();\n        this.jsTree = $(`<div id=\"${this.id}\"></div>`).insertBefore(document.currentScript);\n        this.container = this.jsTree.wrap(`<div class=\"code-file-tree-container\"></div>`);\n\n        const $this = function MDXContent(props = {}) {
  const {wrapper: MDXLayout} = {
    ..._provideComponents(),
    ...props.components
  };
  return MDXLayout ? _jsx(MDXLayout, {
    ...props,
    children: _jsx(_createMdxContent, {
      ...props
    })
  }) : _createMdxContent(props);
}

  default: MDXContent
};
